````math
Table H2b: Question-start results for the single-seed sparse-replay
pilot. Evaluation covers the 12 held-out fold-0 questions with four
samples each. Intervals resample held-out records and do not represent
training-seed replication.

The oracle gap shows that useful alternatives exist in the candidate
set, but the held-out learned critic does not identify them: pooled
AUROC is 0.452, nonzero-effect sign accuracy is 46.3%, and critic
routing captures only 4.9% of oracle headroom. Sparse replay is
similarly inconclusive: Oracle replay equals the best matched-random arm
on question-start accuracy, while FKL replay is below Base.

### H.4 Interpolation

For each matched Qwen3-4B seed and checkpoint
$`s\in\{20,40,60,80,100\}`$, let $`\Delta_{\text{problem},s}`$ and
$`\Delta_{\text{privileged},s}`$ denote the effective LoRA weight
updates from the problem-only and privileged-solution runs. We evaluate
$`\Delta_{s}(\lambda)=(1-\lambda)\Delta_{\text{problem},s}+\lambda\Delta_{\text{privileged},s}`$
at $`\lambda\in\{0,0.5,1,1.5\}`$.

We realize each interpolation exactly with a rank-$`2r`$ adapter. Given
source factors $`A_{\text{problem}},B_{\text{problem}}`$ and
$`A_{\text{privileged}},B_{\text{privileged}}`$, we concatenate
$`A=[A_{\text{problem}};A_{\text{privileged}}]`$ and
$`B=[(1-\lambda)B_{\text{problem}},\lambda B_{\text{privileged}}]`$,
then double both rank and alpha to preserve alpha/$`r`$.
Figure [4](#S8.F4 "Figure 4 ‣ 8 Discussion ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")a–b
reuses the matched
Figure [1](#S1.F1 "Figure 1 ‣ 1 Introduction ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")
evaluations at $`\lambda=0`$ and $`\lambda=1`$ and adds newly evaluated
$`\lambda=0.5`$ and $`\lambda=1.5`$ adapters under the same decoding
protocol. At Step 100, the reconstructed endpoint adapters match the
original effective updates across all 252 target modules, and a fresh
evaluation preserves their ordering.

The privileged solution changes update direction rather than size: at
Step 100, privileged-solution and problem-only updates differ by 39^(∘),
38^(∘), and 50^(∘) at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, while their norm ratios remain
between 0.98 and 1.01
(Figure [H1](#A8.SS4 "H.4 Interpolation ‣ Appendix H Discussion diagnostics ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")).
Both Thinking-OFF and Thinking-ON evaluations use the five-benchmark
Avg@12 suite, temperature 1.0, top-p 1.0, top-k disabled, and a
38,912-token response cap.

The $`\lambda=0`$ and $`\lambda=1`$ rows reuse the matched
Figure [1](#S1.F1 "Figure 1 ‣ 1 Introduction ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")

Table [H3a](#A8.SS4 "H.4 Interpolation ‣ Appendix H Discussion diagnostics ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")
reports the independent fresh Step-100 endpoint check.

At Step 100, Thinking-OFF performance decreases along the
privileged-minus-problem-only direction in every seed, while Thinking-ON
performance remains flat through $`\lambda=0.5`$ and then declines. The
endpoint interpolation therefore erodes the cross-mode gain without
revealing a compensating benefit in the thinking mode used by the
privileged teacher. Because
Table [H3a](#A8.SS4 "H.4 Interpolation ‣ Appendix H Discussion diagnostics ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")
re-evaluates the Step-100 endpoints with fresh samples, its
$`\lambda=0`$ and $`\lambda=1`$ values differ slightly from the reused
Figure [1](#S1.F1 "Figure 1 ‣ 1 Introduction ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")
evaluations in
Table [H3c](#A8.SS4 "H.4 Interpolation ‣ Appendix H Discussion diagnostics ‣ Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models")
(44.37 versus 43.87 and 29.44 versus 28.66 under Thinking OFF; 61.01
versus 60.96 and 60.21 versus 60.25 under Thinking ON). These
differences reflect sampling variation at temperature 1.0, and every
reused value lies within the corresponding fresh seed range.

### H.5 Scope

These experiments are falsification tests, not a joint method
leaderboard. Panels (b), (c), (d), and (f) use one training seed. Panels
(a) and (e) use three. Panels (g-h) average three matched seeds.
Datasets, model scales, and training protocols differ across panels,
while evaluation decoding is fixed within each matched comparison. The
branch audit and replay pilot further separate the existence of useful
local actions from the ability to select and retain them. Together,
these tests narrow several simple implementation explanations without
showing that every alternative OPSD formulation must fail.
````
